          function getCookieValue(e){var o,t,n,a=document.cookie.split(";");for(o=0;o<a.length;o++)if(t=a[o].substr(0,a[o].indexOf("=")),n=a[o].substr(a[o].indexOf("=")+1),t=t.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,""),t==e)return unescape(n);return null}function setCookie(e,o,t){if("undefined"==typeof o){var n=null;if(document.cookie&&""!=document.cookie)for(var a=document.cookie.split(";"),i=0;i<a.length;i++){var l=trimString(a[i]);if(l.substring(0,e.length+1)==e+"="){n=decodeURIComponent(l.substring(e.length+1));break}}return n}t=t||{},null===o&&(o="",t.expires=-1);var p="";if(t.expires&&("number"==typeof t.expires||t.expires.toUTCString)){var u;"number"==typeof t.expires?(u=new Date,u.setTime(u.getTime()+24*t.expires*60*60*1e3)):u=t.expires,p="; expires="+u.toUTCString()}var r=t.path?"; path="+t.path:"",v=t.domain?"; domain="+t.domain:"",s=t.secure?"; secure":"";document.cookie=[e,"=",encodeURIComponent(o),p,r,v,s].join("")}

function changeButtonUrl(product_id){
   var buyNowLink1 = document.getElementById("buyNowLink1");
  if(product_id == ask1que_prdid){
      $("#CategoryTable2").show();
      $("#CategoryTable3").hide();
      $("#CategoryTable4").hide();
      $("#text_ask_div").show();
       $("#trusted_icon_div").show();
      $("#localBlockPrice1 .price-section #price_block_data").html(ask1que_prdid_priceblock);
      buyNowLink1.setAttribute("data-id",product_id); 
      document.getElementById("weProductId").value = ask1que_prdid;  

  }else if(product_id == ask2que_prdid){
   $("#CategoryTable3").show();
    $("#CategoryTable2").hide();
    $("#CategoryTable4").hide();
     $("#text_ask_div").hide();
      $("#trusted_icon_div").show();
    $("#localBlockPrice1 .price-section #price_block_data").html(ask2que_prdid_priceblock);
    buyNowLink1.setAttribute("data-id",product_id);
    document.getElementById("weProductId").value = ask2que_prdid; 
  }else if(product_id == ask5que_prdid){
      $("#CategoryTable4").show();
       $("#CategoryTable2").hide();
       $("#CategoryTable3").hide();
       $("#text_ask_div").hide();
       $("#trusted_icon_div").hide();
       $("#localBlockPrice1 .price-section #price_block_data").html(ask5que_prdid_priceblock);
       buyNowLink1.setAttribute("data-id",product_id); 
       document.getElementById("weProductId").value = ask5que_prdid;
  }
}
function myaskque()
{
       
    var produtid = document.getElementById("buyNowLink1").getAttribute("data-id");  
    if(produtid == ask1que_prdid)
    {   
       var INPUTFIELDCLASS = 'input-field-error';
        var question_obj = document.getElementById("box_expandText");
        var question_input_obj =question_obj;
        question_value   = question_obj.value;  
        question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);     
        var SelectQuestion = document.getElementById("SelectQuestion").value; 
        if(SelectQuestion == "Other")
        {   
            //alert(SelectQuestion); 
            $("#box_expandText").html('');      
            $("#OtherQuestion").show(); 
            $("#div-slect").hide(); 
             $("#label-selected").hide();
            //var SelectQuestion = document.getElementById("SelectQuestion")
            //SelectQuestion.setAttribute('name',"askQuestionOther"); 
            document.getElementById("div-slect").name="askQuestionOther";  
           
            if(question_value.trim().length==0)
            {
                $('.maxaskquestionlength').text('0/200');
                $('.errormsg').text('');
                $('.errormsg').hide();
                question_obj.value = '';  
                question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                return false;                  
            }else if(question_value.trim().length>=25){           
                question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                question_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                $('.errormsg').text("");
                $('.errormsg').hide(); 
            }else{            
                if(question_value.trim().length<=25)
                {
                    question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                    question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                    $('.errormsg').text("Please describe your concern in minimum 25 characters");
                    $('.errormsg').show(); 
                    return false;
                }else{
                    question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                    var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
                }
            }  
        }else if(SelectQuestion != "Other")
        {
             $("#div-slect").show(); 
              $("#label-selected").show();
            var question_obj = document.getElementById("div-slect");
            var question_input_obj =question_obj;
            question_value   = question_obj.value;  
            question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            if(question_value.trim().length==0)
            {
                $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').text('0/300');
                $('.errormsgque').text('');
                $('.errormsgque').hide();
                question_obj.value = '';  
                question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                return false;                  
            }else if(question_value.trim().length>=25){           
                question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                question_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                $('.errormsgque').text("");
                $('.errormsgque').hide(); 
            }else{            
                if(question_value.trim().length<=25)
                {
                    question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                    question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                    $('.errormsgque').text("Please describe your concern in minimum 25 characters");
                    $('.errormsgque').show(); 
                    return false;
                }else{
                    question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                    var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
                }
            }  
        }else{
            $("#OtherQuestion").hide();  
            $("#div-slect").show(); 
             $("#label-selected").show();
            //var SelectQuestion = document.getElementById("SelectQuestion")
            //SelectQuestion.setAttribute('name',"askQuestion");
            document.getElementById("div-slect").name="askQuestion";
        }        
    }
    language_value   = 'English';

  //var askwidget_question_value = encodeURIComponent(question_value);         
  //document.cookie='ask_question_value='+askwidget_question_value+'; path=/; domain='+COOKIES_DOMAIN;   
  //document.cookie='ask_question_value='+askwidget_question_value+'; path=/; domain='+COOKIES_DOMAIN;    

  if(getCookieValue('partnerCode'))
  {
    var partnerName_ask_widget = getCookieValue('partnerCode');
    var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
    form_obj.action = 'https://product.ganeshaspeaks.com/checkout/your-details.action?productId='+produtid+'&gsPosition=HPBB&gsCampaign='+gsCampaign;
   if(produtid == ask1que_prdid){
      document.getElementById("we_buy_now_click").click();
      var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
      var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question').submit();
    } 
  }else{
    var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
    form_obj.action = 'https://product.ganeshaspeaks.com/checkout/your-details.action?productId='+produtid+'&gsPosition=HPBB&gsCampaign='+gsCampaign;
    if(produtid == ask1que_prdid){
      document.getElementById("we_buy_now_click").click();
      var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question').submit();
    } 
  }
  return true;    
}


// hide and show textarea select other option 
$("#OtherQuestion").hide();


function other_select()
{
    var INPUTFIELDCLASS = 'input-field-error';
    var question_obj = document.getElementById("box_expandText");
    var question_input_obj =question_obj;
    question_value   = question_obj.value;  
    question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);     
    var SelectQuestion = document.getElementById("SelectQuestion").value; 
    if(SelectQuestion == "Other")
    {   
        //alert(SelectQuestion); 
        $("#box_expandText").html('');            
        $("#OtherQuestion").show(); 
        $("#label-selected").hide(); 
         $("#div-slect").hide(); 
         $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').hide();
        $('.nrmltxtque').hide();
        //var SelectQuestion = document.getElementById("SelectQuestion");
        //SelectQuestion.setAttribute('name',"askQuestionOther");  
        document.getElementById("div-slect").name="askQuestionOther";
        if(question_value.trim().length==0)
        {
            $('.maxaskquestionlength').text('0/200');
            $('.errormsg').text('');
            $('.errormsg').hide();
            question_obj.value = '';  
            question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            return false;                  
        }else if(question_value.trim().length>=25){
            question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            question_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            $('.errormsg').text("");
            $('.errormsg').hide(); 
        }else{
         
            if(question_value.trim().length<=25)
            {
                question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                $('.errormsg').text("Please describe your concern in minimum 25 characters");
                $('.errormsg').show(); 
                return false;
            }else{
                question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
            }
        }   
       
        /*if(question_value.trim().length==0)
        {
            question_input_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            question_obj.classList.add(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            return false;
        }else{

            question_input_obj.classList.remove(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            var form_obj = document.getElementById('frmask1question');
        }     */ 
       
    }else{
        //console.log("otherselect");
        $("#OtherQuestion").hide();  
        $("#label-selected").show(); 
        $("#div-slect").show(); 
        $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').show();
        $('.nrmltxtque').show();       
        var SelectQuestion = document.getElementById("SelectQuestion")
        //SelectQuestion.setAttribute('name',"askQuestion");  
        document.getElementById("div-slect").name="askQuestion";
        var textarea = $("#div-slect");     
        var val = SelectQuestion.value;
        if (val.charAt(val.length-1) != " ") {
            val += " ";
        }
        textarea.focus().val("").val(val);
    }
}

$( "#SelectQuestion" ).change(function() { 
 var val = $("#SelectQuestion").val();    
 $("#div-slect").html(val);
 if(val=="Other"){        
    $("#div-slect").hide();    
} else {       
 $("#div-slect").show();    

}
});


// Max characters length in textarea 
$('#box_expandText').keyup(function(e){
    var length = $(this).val().length;   
    if (length > 0) {
        $(this).addClass('data-edits');

    }else{
        $(this).removeClass('data-edits');
    }
    var text = $(this).val();
    var textLength = text.length;
    if (textLength >= (300+1))
    {
        $(this).val($(this).val().substr(0, 300));
        $('.errormsg').text("Sorry, only 300 characters are allowed");
        $('.errormsg').show();
        $('.maxaskquestionlength').text($(this).val().length+'/300');
        e.preventDefault();
    }else{
        $('.maxaskquestionlength').text(textLength+'/300');
        $('.errormsg').text('');
        $('.errormsg').hide();
    }   
});
$("#box_expandText").bind("paste", function(e){
    var length = $(this).val().length;
    if (length > 0) {
        $(this).addClass('data-edits');
    }else
    {
        $(this).removeClass('data-edits');
    }
    var text = $(this).val();
    var textLength = text.length;
    if (textLength >= (300+1))
    {
        $('.errormsg').text("Sorry, only 300 characters are allowed");
        $('.errormsg').show();
        $(this).val($(this).val().substr(0, 300));
        $('.maxaskquestionlength').text($(this).val().length+'/300');
        e.preventDefault();
    }else{
        $('.maxaskquestionlength').text(textLength+'/300');
        $('.errormsg').text('');
        $('.errormsg').hide();
   } 
});

$('#div-slect').keyup(function(e){
    var length = $(this).val().length;   
    if (length > 0) {
        $(this).addClass('data-edits');
    }else{
        $(this).removeClass('data-edits');
    }
    var text = $(this).val();
    var textLength = text.length;
    if (textLength >= (300+1))
    {
        $(this).val($(this).val().substr(0, 300));
        $('.errormsgque').text("Sorry, only 300 characters are allowed");
        $('.errormsgque').show();
        $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').text($(this).val().length+'/300');
        e.preventDefault();
    }else{
        $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').text(textLength+'/300');
        $('.errormsgque').text('');
        $('.errormsgque').hide();
    }   
});
$("#div-slect").bind("paste", function(e){
    var length = $(this).val().length;
    if (length > 0) {
        $(this).addClass('data-edits');
    }else
    {
        $(this).removeClass('data-edits');
    }
    var text = $(this).val();
    var textLength = text.length;
    if (textLength >= (300+1))
    {
        $('.errormsgque').text("Sorry, only 300 characters are allowed");
        $('.errormsgque').show();
        $(this).val($(this).val().substr(0, 300));
        $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').text($(this).val().length+'/300');
        e.preventDefault();
    }else{
        $('.maxaskquestionlengthque').text(textLength+'/300');
        $('.errormsgque').text('');
        $('.errormsgque').hide();
   } 
});

  // This function is call for getting Astrologer Data from VCDial call in gsv-function.php file
  function vcdial_data_fun()
  {
    var date = new Date();
    var time = date.getTime();
    //var astro_json_url = "https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/3QNjAYjisTpj5/data/vcdial_astro/AstrologerLoginStatus.json?"+time;
    //var astro_json_url = POMSITEPATH + "astrologersStatus.do?"+time;    
    var astro_json_url = "https://product.ganeshaspeaks.com/astrologersStatus.do?"+time;    
    jQuery.getJSON(astro_json_url, function (data) {   
      jQuery.ajaxSetup({cache: false});
      jQuery.ajax({
        method: "POST",
        url : "/ajax/",      

        data : {'data' : data,'action':'my_unique_action','method_name':'live_vcdial_get_data'},
        success:function(data) {
          /*console.log("data");
          console.log(data);*/
          jQuery('.astrologers_availability_id_cls').html(data);
        },
        error: function(errorThrown){
          //alert("Error");
        }
      });
    });
  }

setInterval(function(){ vcdial_data_fun(); }, 7000); 

  function astrologer_availibility(){
    var live_astrologer_list_arr = [];
    var status_obj_live_astrologer_list = {};   
    var data1 = [];
    var data2 = [];
    var data3 = [];
    var data4 = [];
    var data5 = [];
    var avaible_count = 0;
    var busy_count = 0;
    var notavailbe_count = 0;
    jQuery.ajaxSetup({cache: false});
    var date = new Date();
    var time = date.getTime();
    //var astro_json_url = "https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/3QNjAYjisTpj5/data/vcdial_astro/AstrologerLoginStatus.json?"+time;
    //var astro_json_url = POMSITEPATH + "astrologersStatus.do?"+time;    
    var astro_json_url = "https://product.ganeshaspeaks.com/astrologersStatus.do?"+time;
    jQuery.getJSON(astro_json_url, function (data) { 
    //jQuery.getJSON("https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/3QNjAYjisTpj5/data/vcdial_astro/AstrologerLoginStatus.json", function (data) {
    //$.getJSON(ASTROLOGER_STATUS_DATA_ROOT +"AstrologerLoginStatus.json", function (data) {
      var astroList ='';
      jQuery.ajaxSetup({cache: false});
      var second = '';
      var name = '';
      var status  = '';
      var status_avail = [];
      var status_avail_tot = '';
      for (var i = 0, len = data.length; i < len; i++) {
        name =  '';
        name = data[i].name;
        live_astrologer_list_arr.push(name);
        status_obj_live_astrologer_list[name] = data[i].status;        
      }
      //console.log("datalength");
      //console.log(data.length);
      
      
      var k=0,j=0,tot_cnt=6;
      jQuery.each(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj, function( talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_index, talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value ) {
        //alert(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['name']);
        //alert(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_index);

        //if(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_index <= 6){
          var status = '';
          var user_image = '';
          var extension_number = '';
          var experience = '';
          var display_name = '';
          var name = talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['name'];
          var display_name = talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['display_name'];
    
          if(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['user_image']!=''){
            user_image = talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['user_image'];
          }else{
            user_image = IMAGE_URL+'userimg.jpg';
          }
          if(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['experience']!=''){
            experience = talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['experience'];
          }else{
            experience = '';
          }
          if(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['extension_number']!=''){
            extension_number = talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['extension_number'];
          }else{
            extension_number = '';
          }

          if($.inArray(talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['name'],live_astrologer_list_arr)!=-1){
            if(status_obj_live_astrologer_list[talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['name']]!=''){
              status = status_obj_live_astrologer_list[talk_astrologer_list_json_obj_value['name']];
               if (status == 'Available') {
                status_avail.push(status);
               }
            }else{

<p><a class="read-more-link" href="https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com" style="display: inline; margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;" title="Astrology">Astrology</a> or Jyotisha is one of the oldest sciences, which takes into consideration the movement of the heavenly bodies and the inter-connection between them. This science is that fine line between uncertainty and enlightenment, mystery and realisation. As Astrology is a Vedanga, it has answers to almost all our problems. Apart from a predictive science, Astrology can also be called an intuitive art. Astrology primarily is of two types: Sayan (Western) and Nirayan (Vedic). Some other types are Chinese, Mayan, Burmese, Tibetan, Hellenistic. Numerology on the other hand deals with numbers and the deeper aspects. </p>

<script>
    var error_class_text = 'red'; 
    var INPUTFIELDCLASS = 'input-field-error';
    var global_animation_flag = 0;
    var global_enquire_animation_flag = 0;

    var daily_horoscope_path = 'https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/horoscopes/daily-horoscope/';
    var tomorrow_horoscope_path = 'https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/horoscopes/tomorrow-horoscope/';
    var yesterday_horoscope_path = 'https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/horoscopes/yesterday-horoscope/';


    var panchang_city = 'Mumbai';
    var panchang_country = 'India';

    function validation(obj,type,e)
        {
     
        if (e.keyCode == 9 || e.keyCode == 27 || (e.keyCode == 65) || (e.keyCode >= 35 && e.keyCode <= 39 ) || e.keyCode == 16|| e.keyCode == 32) {
         e.preventDefault();
         return false;
      }else if(e.keyCode == 13){
           $('.signup').click();

     }else{  
      if(type=='.gender-error'){


        $(type).css("border","none");
        $(type).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
        

      }else if(type=='.date-select #date'){

      if($.trim($(type).val()).length==0)
      {
        $('.date-select .select-dropdown').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

      }else{
        $('.date-select .select-dropdown').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }
      }else if(type=='.month-select #month'){

        if($.trim($(type).val()).length==0)
      {
        $('.month-select .select-dropdown').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }else{
        $('.month-select .select-dropdown').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }

      }else if(type=='.year-select #year'){


         if($.trim($(type).val()).length==0)
      {
        $('.year-select .select-dropdown').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }else{
        $('.year-select .select-dropdown').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }
      }else if(type=='.code-select #code'){


         if($.trim($(type).val()).length==0)
      {
        $('.code-select .select-dropdown').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }else{
        $('.code-select .select-dropdown').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }
      }else if(type=='mobile_number'){
           if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
          {
           $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

          }else{
            $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          }
          $("label[for='mobile_number']").html('Mobile number');
          $("label[for='mobile_number']").css('color','');

      }else if(type=='email_address'){
           if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
          {
           $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

          }else{
            $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          }
          $("label[for='email_address']").html('Email Address');
          $("label[for='email_address']").css('color','');

      }else{
      
      if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
      {
       $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }else{
        $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }
    }   
    }     

    }    


    function evalidation(obj,type,e)
        {
     
        if (e.keyCode == 9 || e.keyCode == 27 || (e.keyCode == 65) || (e.keyCode >= 35 && e.keyCode <= 39 ) || e.keyCode == 16|| e.keyCode == 32) {
         e.preventDefault();
         return false;
      }else if(e.keyCode == 13){
           $('.createInquiryOrder').click();

     }else{  
      if(type=='registerUser_parameter_emailId'){
           if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
          {
           $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

          }else{
            $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          }
          $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").html('Email Address');
          $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").css('color','');
          
      }else if(type=='registerUser_parameter_contactNo'){
          if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
          {
           $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

          }else{
            $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          }
          $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").html('Contact Number');
          $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").css('color','');
      }else if(type=='msgenquire'){

        if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
          {
           $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

          }else{
            $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          }
          $('.msgenquire').hide();
          $('.msgenquire').html('');
          
      }else{
      
      if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
      {
       $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }else{
        $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
      }
    }   
    }     

    }    

    function home_zodiac_horoscope_fun(object){
        var val = $(object).val();  
        var option = $(object).children('option')[1];
        var zodiacName = $(option).attr('data-zodiac');
        if($.trim(val).length > 0){
          if(val=='tomorrow'){
              window.location.href= tomorrow_horoscope_path+zodiacName+'/';    
          }else if(val=='yesterday'){
              window.location.href= yesterday_horoscope_path+zodiacName+'/';
          }else{
              window.location.href= daily_horoscope_path+zodiacName+'/';
          }
        }
  
}

	 $('.datepicker_fetival').pickadate({

        //Today and clear

        clear: 'Clear',

             $(document.activeElement).blur();
          $('.datepicker_fetival').pickadate();
        },
        //The format to show on the `input` element
        format: 'dd/m/yyyy'

function getmonthyearfromdatepicker(month,year){
     var m=new Array('January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December');
    mon=m[month];
      $(".festival_date").text(mon+' '+year);
    var data = {'month' : mon,'method_name':'get_month_of_festival','year1':year,'action':'my_unique_action','templateName':'Home' }

  $.ajax({
        url : "/ajax/",
        method: "POST",
        data : data,
        success : function(data){         $('.home_month_festival').html(data);
        } 

    });


}
function refresh_month_function()
{
    $(".festival_calender .picker__select--month").on('change',function(){
        var month=$(".festival_calender .picker__select--month").val();
        var year=$(".festival_calender .picker__year").html();
        getmonthyearfromdatepicker(month,year);
        refresh_month_function(); // binding event again ... 
        refresh_year_function(); // binding event again ... 
    });


}
function refresh_year_function()
{
    $(".festival_calender .picker__select--year").on('change',function(){
    var month=$(".festival_calender .picker__select--month").val();
    var year=$(".festival_calender .picker__year").html();
    getmonthyearfromdatepicker(month,year);
    refresh_year_function(); // binding event again ... 
    refresh_month_function(); // binding event again ... 
    });
   
}

    function removeTags(str){
        var txt = str;
        var rex = /(<([^>]+)>)/ig;
        return txt.replace(rex , "");
    }

    function titleCase(string) {
        return string.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + string.slice(1);
    }

    function ksort(obj){
        var keys = Object.keys(obj).sort()
                , sortedObj = {};

        for(var i in keys) {
            sortedObj[keys[i]] = obj[keys[i]];
        }

        return sortedObj;
    }

    

function isNumberKey(evt){
    var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode
    if (charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57))
        return false;
    return true;
}

    $('.datepicker_panchang').change(function() { 

    var m=new Array('','Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec');
    var date = $(".datepicker_panchang").val();

    var n=date.substring(0,date.indexOf("/"));
    
    var day=n;
    var M=date.split("/");
    var mon=M[1];

    var month=m[mon];
    var year=M[2];

    var datepicker_date = year+"-"+mon+"-"+day;
    $('.setDate').text(day+" "+month+","+year);

     var data = {'datepicker_date' : datepicker_date,'panchang_city':panchang_city,'panchang_country':panchang_country,'action':'my_unique_action','method_name':'get_current_panchang_detail','templateName':'home' }

                $.ajax({
                  
                  url : "/ajax/",
                  method: "POST",
                  data : data,
                  success : function(data){
                    $('.home_panchanglist').html(data);
                  

                  } 

    }) 
  });
 
	




function reset_event(){
    $('.redirect_home_horoscope .redirect_home_horoscope').off('change').on('change',function(){
        $link = $(this).val();
        window.location.href = $link;
    });      
}



            function createInquiryOrder(){
              document.getElementById('enquiremessage').style.display='none';
              var productId = $('#enquireid').val();
            
              $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
              $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
              $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
              $('.msgenquire').hide();
              $('.msgenquire').html('');

               $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_name']").removeClass('animationSandbox');
               $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").removeClass('animationSandbox');
               $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").removeClass('animationSandbox');
               $("#registerUser_parameter_comment").removeClass('animationSandbox');



              var name = $('#registerUser_parameter_name').val();
              var emailId = $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').val();
              var mobileNo = $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').val();
              var comment = $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').val();
          
              var letters = /^[A-Za-z0-9 ]+$/;  
              var reg = /^[_a-zA-Z0-9-]+(\.[_a-zA-Z0-9-]+)*@[a-zA-Z0-9-]+(\.[a-zA-Z0-9-]+)*(\.[a-zA-Z]{2,4})$/;
              var token=0;

                if($.trim(name).length==0)
                {  
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_name').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                  if(global_enquire_animation_flag==0){
                      $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_name']").addClass('animationSandbox');
                  }    
                  token=1; 
                }
                
                if($.trim(emailId).length==0){
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                  if(global_enquire_animation_flag==0){
                      $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").addClass('animationSandbox');

                  }
                   $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").html('Email Address');
                    $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").css('color','');
                  token=1;
                }else if(!reg.test(emailId)){
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                  $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").html('Enter valid email address');
                  $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").css('color',error_class_text);
                 token=1;
               }else{
                  $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").html('Email Address');
                  $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_emailId']").css('color','');
               }

               if($.trim(mobileNo).length==0){
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                  if(global_enquire_animation_flag==0){
                      $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").addClass('animationSandbox');

                  }
                   $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").html('Contact Number');
                   $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").css('color','');
                  token=1;
                }else if($.trim(mobileNo).length<10||$.trim(mobileNo).length>12||isNaN(mobileNo))
                {   
                 
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                  $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").html('Enter valid contact number');
                  $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").css('color',error_class_text);
                  token=1;
                }else{
                     $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").html('Contact Number');
                     $("label[for='registerUser_parameter_contactNo']").css('color','');


                }

                 if($.trim(comment).length==0)
                { 
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS); 
                  $('.msgenquire').hide();
                  $('.msgenquire').html('');
                  if(global_enquire_animation_flag==0){
                      $("#registerUser_parameter_comment").addClass('animationSandbox');

                  }
                 
                  token=1; 
                }else if(comment.length>3000){
                  $('.msgenquire').show();
                  $('.msgenquire').html('Query should not exceed 3000 characters');
                 
                  $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                 
                  token=1; 
                }else{
                   $('.msgenquire').hide();
                  $('.msgenquire').html('');
                }

            if(!token){
                var url='https://product.ganeshaspeaks.com/createNewOrder.do';
                var parameter='productId='+productId+'&name='+name+'& inquiry=true&emailId='+emailId+'&mobileNo='+mobileNo+'&comment='+comment;
                parameter=parameter.split("#038;").join("");
                var xhttp=new XMLHttpRequest();xhttp.onreadystatechange=function(){
                if(xhttp.readyState==4){
                    document.getElementById('enquiremessage').style.display='block';
                    clear_enquire_form('clearsubmit');
                    return false;
                }};
                xhttp.open("POST",url,true);
                xhttp.setRequestHeader("Content-type","application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
                xhttp.send(parameter);
            }else{
                var anim = $('.animation-value').val();
                enquireAnim(anim);
            }
        }

        function clear_enquire_form(parameter){
            $('#registerUser_parameter_name').val("");
            $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').val("");
            $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').val("");
            $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').val("");

             $('#registerUser_parameter_name').blur();
            $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').blur();
            $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').blur();
            $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').blur();

          $('#registerUser_parameter_name').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          $('#registerUser_parameter_emailId').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          $('#registerUser_parameter_contactNo').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          $('#registerUser_parameter_comment').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
          $('.msgenquire').hide();
          $('.msgenquire').html('');
          if(parameter!='clearsubmit')
          {  
            global_enquire_animation_flag=0;
          }

          
        }
        function clear_message(){
           document.getElementById('enquiremessage').style.display='none';
        }
        function title_change(msg,productid){
          $('.msgtxt').html(msg);
          $('#enquireid').val(productid);
          clear_enquire_form('clearfirst');
          clear_message();
        }

  $('#ask-question').keyup(function(e){
      var length = $(this).val().length;
  if (length > 0) {
    $(this).addClass('data-edits');
  } else {
    $(this).removeClass('data-edits');
  }
             var text = $(this).val();
           var textLength = text.length;

  if (textLength >= (300+1)) {
  
               
               $(this).val($(this).val().substr(0, 300));
               $('.errormsg').text("Sorry, only 300 characters are allowed");
               $('.errormsg').show();
               $('.maxaskquestionlength').text($(this).val().length+'/300');
               e.preventDefault();
             }else{
              $('.maxaskquestionlength').text(textLength+'/300');
          $('.errormsg').text('');
              $('.errormsg').hide();
           }   
});
  $("#ask-question").bind("paste", function(e){
     var length = $(this).val().length;
  if (length > 0) {
    $(this).addClass('data-edits');
  } else {
    $(this).removeClass('data-edits');
  }
             var text = $(this).val();
           var textLength = text.length;

  if (textLength >= (300+1)) {
               $('.errormsg').text("Sorry, only 300 characters are allowed");
               $('.errormsg').show();
               $(this).val($(this).val().substr(0, 300));
               $('.maxaskquestionlength').text($(this).val().length+'/300');
               e.preventDefault();
             }else{
              $('.maxaskquestionlength').text(textLength+'/300');
          $('.errormsg').text('');
              $('.errormsg').hide();
           } 
} );

   function default_price(){
          var pricebluck = '';
          var SITE_URL = 'https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com';
          var divs = document.getElementsByClassName('price3');
          for (var i = 0; i < divs.length; i++) {

            var productId = divs[i].getAttribute('data-id');
            pricebluck+=productId+',';
         }
             jQuery.ajax(
             {
               type: "POST",
               async: true,
               data : { productIds : pricebluck,action:'my_unique_action',method_name:'productPriceArray'},
                 url: SITE_URL+"/ajax/",
                 success: function(request) {
                    if(request){

                    var pricebluckresponse = jQuery.parseJSON($.trim(request));
                    var divs = document.getElementsByClassName('price3');
                    var pricebluck1 = '';
                    for (var i = 0; i < divs.length; i++) {
                      var productId= divs[i].getAttribute('data-id');
                      var priceobj = document.getElementsByClassName('priceid-'+productId);
                      if(pricebluckresponse[productId]){
                        $('.priceid-'+productId).html(pricebluckresponse[productId]);
                      }else{

                        pricebluck1+=productId+',';

                      }
                     
                    }
                     if(pricebluck1){
                             jQuery.ajax(
                             {
                               type: "POST",
                               async: true,
                               data : { productIds : pricebluck,action:'my_unique_action',method_name:'productPriceArray'},
                                 url: SITE_URL+"/ajaxprice/",
                                 success: function(request) {
                                    arr = pricebluck1.split(',');
                                    var pricebluckresponse = jQuery.parseJSON($.trim(request));
                                    for (var i = 0; i < (arr.length-1); i++) {
                                      var productId= arr[i] ;
                                      $('.priceid-'+productId).html(pricebluckresponse[productId]);
                                    }
                                }
                              });
                      }
                  }else{

                    jQuery.ajax(
             {
               type: "POST",
               async: true,
               data : { productIds : pricebluck,action:'my_unique_action',method_name:'productPriceArray'},
                 url: SITE_URL+"/ajaxprice/",
                 success: function(request) {
                    
                    var pricebluckresponse = jQuery.parseJSON($.trim(request));
                    var divs = document.getElementsByClassName('price3');
                    for (var i = 0; i < divs.length; i++) {
                      var productId= divs[i].getAttribute('data-id');
                      var priceobj = document.getElementsByClassName('priceid-'+productId);
                      $('.priceid-'+productId).html(pricebluckresponse[productId]);
                    }
                }
              });

                  }
                }
              });
          }
           default_price();

</script>

        });

    $('.login').on('click',function()
    {
        $(".error_class_emailaddress").css("display","none");
        $(".error_class_password").css("display","none");
        $('#email').css("border","1px solid #ddd");
        $('#password_em').css("border","1px solid #ddd");


        var email=$("#email").val();
        var pass=$("#password_em").val();
        var reg = /^[_a-zA-Z0-9-]+(\.[_a-zA-Z0-9-]+)*@[a-zA-Z0-9-]+(\.[a-zA-Z0-9-]+)*(\.[a-zA-Z]{2,4})$/;
        var token=0;
        //   alert(email+pass);
        if($.trim(email).length == 0)
        {
            $(".msgtoplogin").html('');
            $('.msgtoplogin').hide();
            $('#email').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            if(global_toplogin_animation_flag==0){
                $("#email").addClass('animationSandbox');
            }
            token=1;
            //return false;
        }else if (!reg.test(email))
        {
            $('#email').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            $(".msgtoplogin").html('Enter valid email address');;
            $('.msgtoplogin').show();

            token=1;

        }else{
            $(".msgtoplogin").html('');
            $('.msgtoplogin').hide();
        }
        if($.trim(pass).length == 0)
        {
            $('#password_em').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            if(global_toplogin_animation_flag==0){
                $("#password_em").addClass('animationSandbox');
            }
            token=1;
        }
        //var url=PRODUCT_SITE_URL+"loginCheck.do?publicEmailId="+email+"&password="+pass+'&sdfsdfsd';
        var url=PRODUCT_SITE_URL+"loginCheck.do";
        if(!token)
        {
            //$.ajaxSetup({ cache: false });
            $.post(url,{'publicEmailId':email,'password':pass},function(data){
                if(data!='emailValidationRequire'){
                    if(data!='error'){
                        data=JSON.parse(data);
                        $(".loginuser").html('<span class="logged-user">Welcome <a style="color:#3EABBB; font-weight:normal;margin-right:5px" href="">'+data.name+'</a>|<span class="logoutacc" style="margin-left:5px;" onclick="deletelogincookie()">Logout</span></span>');
                        $('.loginuser').show()
                        $('.wloginuser').hide();
                        $(".mloginuser").html('<div class="user-avatar"><a href="https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/my-ganeshaspeaks-profile/" title=""><span class="circle">'+data.name[0]+'</span></a></div><div class="user-body"><a href="https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/my-ganeshaspeaks-profile/" title="" class="user-name"><span class="name truncate-line">Welcome! '+data.name+'</span></a><a href="https://www.ganeshaspeaks.com/my-ganeshaspeaks-profile/" title="" class="user-mail"><span class="email truncate-line">'+data.emailId+'</span></a><a style="color: #3eabbb;" href="javascript:void(0);" onclick="deletelogincookie();" title="" class="user-mail"><span class="email truncate-line">Logout</span></a></div>');

                        $(".mloginuser").show();
                        $(".mwloginuser").hide();
                        document.cookie='userName='+data.name+'; path=/; domain='+COOKIES_DOMAIN;
                        document.cookie='userData='+JSON.stringify(data)+'; path=/; domain='+COOKIES_DOMAIN;
                        document.cookie='userIdentity='+data.userIdentity+';  path=/; domain='+COOKIES_DOMAIN;
                        if(data.custId) {
                          var custid = data.custId;
                          if(data.name) {
                            if(data.name.indexOf(" ") >= 0) {
                              var firstname = data.name.split(" ")[0];
                              var lastname = data.name.split(" ")[1];
                            } else {
                              var firstname = data.name;
                            }
                          }
                          $("#we_user_id_lgn").val(data.custId);
                          $("#we_first_name_lgn").val(data.name.split(" ")[0]);
                          $("#we_last_name_lgn").val(data.name.split(" ")[1]);
                          $("#we_email_lgn").val(data.emailId);
                          $("#we_gender_lgn").val(data.gender);
                          if(data.birthDate) {
                            if(data.birthDate.length == 8) {
                              $("#we_year_lgn").val(data.birthDate.substring(4,8));
                              $("#we_month_lgn").val(data.birthDate.substring(2,4));
                              $("#we_date_lgn").val(data.birthDate.substring(0,2));
                            }
                          }
                          if(data.sunSign) {
                            $("#we_sunsign_lgn").val(data.sunSign);
                          }
                          if(data.sunSign) {
                            $("#we_moonsign_lgn").val(data.moonSign);
                          }
                          if(data.sunSign) {
                            $("#we_ascendant_lgn").val(data.ascendant);
                          }
                          $("#we_mobile_lgn").val(data.cellNo);
                          $("#we_lgn_btn").click();
                        }

                        var login_userdetail_data = {'email_address' : data.emailId,'action':'my_unique_action','method_name':'userdata_save_fun'}
                        $.ajax({
                            url : "/ajax/",
                            method: "POST",
                            data : login_userdetail_data,
                            success : function(data){
                                if(data){
                                    window.location.reload();
                                }
                            }

                        });





                    }else{

                        $(".msgtoplogin").html('Please enter correct credentials');
                        $('.msgtoplogin').show();
                    }
                }else{

                    $(".msgtoplogin").html('Your email is still not verified');
                    $('.msgtoplogin').show();
                }

            });
        }else{
            var anim = $('.animation-value').val();
            toploginAnim(anim);

            $('#last_name').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            token=1;

        if(date.length==0||date=='Date')

            $('.date-select .select-dropdown').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

            $("label[for='mobile_number']").html('Mobile Number');
            $("label[for='mobile_number']").css('color','');

                    window.location.assign(finance_link);

    //Check to see if the window is top if not then display button
    $(window).scroll(function(){
        if ($(this).scrollTop() > 800) {
            $('.scrollToTop').fadeIn();
        } else {
            $('.scrollToTop').fadeOut();
        }
    });

    //Click event to scroll to top
    $('.scrollToTop').click(function(){

    $('.subscribe').on('click',function(){

        $(".error_class_subscribe").css("display","none");

        //$('#subscribe_email').css("border","none");
        $('#subscribe_email').removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
        $("label[for='subscribe_email']").removeClass('animationSandbox');


        var subscribe_email = $("#subscribe_email").val();

        var reg = /^[_a-zA-Z0-9-]+(\.[_a-zA-Z0-9-]+)*@[a-zA-Z0-9-]+(\.[a-zA-Z0-9-]+)*(\.[a-zA-Z]{2,4})$/;
        var token=0;

        if($.trim(subscribe_email).length==0){
            $('#subscribe_email').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            $("label[for='subscribe_email']").html('Email Address');
            $("label[for='subscribe_email']").css('color','');
            if(global_footersubscribe_animation_flag==0){
                $("label[for='subscribe_email']").addClass('animationSandbox');
            }
            token=1;
        }else if(!reg.test(subscribe_email)){
            $('#subscribe_email').addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
            $("label[for='subscribe_email']").html('Enter valid email address');
            $("label[for='subscribe_email']").css('color',error_class_text);
            token=1;
        }else{
            $("label[for='subscribe_email']").html('Email Address');
            $("label[for='subscribe_email']").css('color','');
        }

    $('.nav-top-main .s3>a').click(function () {
        var id = $(this).attr('id');
        var containerId = 'empty';
        var containerIdFor = 'empty';
        if ($(this).next().attr('id')) {
            containerId = $(this).next().attr('id');
            containerIdFor = $(this).next().attr('for');
        }
        if ($('.nav-top-main .s3 a').each(function () {
                    $(this).removeClass('active');
                }) && $('.nav-dropdown-section').each(function (index, element) {
                    if (id != $(element).attr('data-for')) {
                        $(element).slideUp();
                    }
                })) {

            $(this).toggleClass('active');

            if (id == 'navSearch') {
                $('#navSearchContainer').slideToggle();

            }else if (id == 'navFreeReports') {
                $('#navFreeReportsContainer').slideToggle();
            }else if (id == 'navsearch') {

            }



        }
    });

    function compatibilitysign(sign1,sign2)
    {
        if((sign1.length >0) && (sign2.length>0)){
            var lsign1 = sign1.toLowerCase();
            var lsign2 = sign2.toLowerCase();

            var ZODIAC_SIGNS_COMPATIBILITY_URL  = ZODIAC_SIGNS_COMPATIBILITY_PATH+lsign1+'-'+lsign2+'/';
            window.location.assign(ZODIAC_SIGNS_COMPATIBILITY_URL);
        }
        return false;

    }




    function getCookieValue(e){var o,t,n,a=document.cookie.split(";");for(o=0;o<a.length;o++)if(t=a[o].substr(0,a[o].indexOf("=")),n=a[o].substr(a[o].indexOf("=")+1),t=t.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,""),t==e)return unescape(n);return null}function setCookie(e,o,t){if("undefined"==typeof o){var n=null;if(document.cookie&&""!=document.cookie)for(var a=document.cookie.split(";"),i=0;i<a.length;i++){var l=trimString(a[i]);if(l.substring(0,e.length+1)==e+"="){n=decodeURIComponent(l.substring(e.length+1));break}}return n}t=t||{},null===o&&(o="",t.expires=-1);var p="";if(t.expires&&("number"==typeof t.expires||t.expires.toUTCString)){var u;"number"==typeof t.expires?(u=new Date,u.setTime(u.getTime()+24*t.expires*60*60*1e3)):u=t.expires,p="; expires="+u.toUTCString()}var r=t.path?"; path="+t.path:"",v=t.domain?"; domain="+t.domain:"",s=t.secure?"; secure":"";document.cookie=[e,"=",encodeURIComponent(o),p,r,v,s].join("")}

    (function (factory) {
        if (typeof define === 'function' && define.amd) {
            // AMD
            define(['jquery'], factory);
        } else if (typeof exports === 'object') {
            // CommonJS
            module.exports = factory(require('jquery'));
        } else {
            // Browser globals
            factory(jQuery);
        }
    }(function ($) {
        'use strict';

        var readmore = 'readmore',
                defaults = {
                    speed: 100,
                    collapsedHeight: 200,
                    heightMargin: 16,
                    moreLink: '<a href="#">Read More</a>',
                    lessLink: '<a href="#">Read Less</a>',
                    embedCSS: true,
                    blockCSS: 'display: block; width: 100%;',
                    startOpen: false,

                    // callbacks
                    blockProcessed: function () {},
                    beforeToggle: function () {},
                    afterToggle: function () {}
                },
                cssEmbedded = {},
                uniqueIdCounter = 0;

        function debounce(func, wait, immediate) {
            var timeout;

            return function () {
                var context = this,
                        args = arguments;
                var later = function () {
                    timeout = null;
                    if (!immediate) {
                        func.apply(context, args);
                    }
                };
                var callNow = immediate && !timeout;

                clearTimeout(timeout);
                timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);

                if (callNow) {
                    func.apply(context, args);
                }
            };
        }

        function uniqueId(prefix) {
            var id = ++uniqueIdCounter;

            return String(prefix == null ? 'rmjs-' : prefix) + id;
        }

        function setBoxHeights(element) {
            var el = element.clone().css({
                        height: 'auto',
                        width: element.width(),
                        maxHeight: 'none',
                        overflow: 'hidden'
                    }).insertAfter(element),
                    expandedHeight = el.outerHeight(),
                    cssMaxHeight = parseInt(el.css({
                        maxHeight: ''
                    }).css('max-height').replace(/[^-\d\.]/g, ''), 10),
                    defaultHeight = element.data('defaultHeight');

            el.remove();

            var collapsedHeight = cssMaxHeight || element.data('collapsedHeight') || defaultHeight;

            // Store our measurements.
            element.data({
                expandedHeight: expandedHeight,
                maxHeight: cssMaxHeight,
                collapsedHeight: collapsedHeight
            })
            // and disable any `max-height` property set in CSS
                    .css({
                        maxHeight: 'none'
                    });
        }

        var resizeBoxes = debounce(function () {
            $('[data-readmore]').each(function () {
                var current = $(this),
                        isExpanded = (current.attr('aria-expanded') === 'true');

                setBoxHeights(current);

                current.css({
                    height: current.data((isExpanded ? 'expandedHeight' : 'collapsedHeight'))
                });
            });
        }, 100);

        function embedCSS(options) {
            if (!cssEmbedded[options.selector]) {
                var styles = ' ';

                if (options.embedCSS && options.blockCSS !== '') {
                    styles += options.selector + ' + [data-readmore-toggle], ' +
                            options.selector + '[data-readmore]{' +
                            options.blockCSS +
                            '}';
                }

                // Include the transition CSS even if embedCSS is false
                styles += options.selector + '[data-readmore]{' +
                        'transition: height ' + options.speed + 'ms;' +
                        'overflow: hidden;' +
                        '}';

                (function (d, u) {
                    var css = d.createElement('style');
                    css.type = 'text/css';

                    if (css.styleSheet) {
                        css.styleSheet.cssText = u;
                    } else {
                        css.appendChild(d.createTextNode(u));
                    }

                    d.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(css);
                }(document, styles));

                cssEmbedded[options.selector] = true;
            }
        }

        function Readmore(element, options) {
            this.element = element;

            this.options = $.extend({}, defaults, options);

            embedCSS(this.options);

            this._defaults = defaults;
            this._name = readmore;

            this.init();

            // IE8 chokes on `window.addEventListener`, so need to test for support.
            if (window.addEventListener) {
                // Need to resize boxes when the page has fully loaded.
                window.addEventListener('load', resizeBoxes);
                window.addEventListener('resize', resizeBoxes);
            } else {
                window.attachEvent('load', resizeBoxes);
                window.attachEvent('resize', resizeBoxes);
            }
        }


        Readmore.prototype = {
            init: function () {
                var current = $(this.element);

                current.data({
                    defaultHeight: this.options.collapsedHeight,
                    heightMargin: this.options.heightMargin
                });

                setBoxHeights(current);

                var collapsedHeight = current.data('collapsedHeight'),
                        heightMargin = current.data('heightMargin');

                if (current.outerHeight(true) <= collapsedHeight + heightMargin) {
                    // The block is shorter than the limit, so there's no need to truncate it.
                    if (this.options.blockProcessed && typeof this.options.blockProcessed === 'function') {
                        this.options.blockProcessed(current, false);
                    }
                    return true;
                } else {
                    var id = current.attr('id') || uniqueId(),
                            useLink = this.options.startOpen ? this.options.lessLink : this.options.moreLink;

                    current.attr({
                        'data-readmore': '',
                        'aria-expanded': this.options.startOpen,
                        'id': id
                    });

                    current.after($(useLink)
                            .on('click', (function (_this) {
                                return function (event) {
                                    _this.toggle(this, current[0], event);
                                };
                            })(this))
                            .attr({
                                'data-readmore-toggle': id,
                                'aria-controls': id
                            }));

                    if (!this.options.startOpen) {
                        current.css({
                            height: collapsedHeight
                        });
                    }

                    if (this.options.blockProcessed && typeof this.options.blockProcessed === 'function') {
                        this.options.blockProcessed(current, true);
                    }
                }
            },

            toggle: function (trigger, element, event) {
                if (event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                }

                if (!trigger) {
                    trigger = $('[aria-controls="' + this.element.id + '"]')[0];
                }

                if (!element) {
                    element = this.element;
                }

                var $element = $(element),
                        newHeight = '',
                        newLink = '',
                        expanded = false,
                        collapsedHeight = $element.data('collapsedHeight');

                if ($element.height() <= collapsedHeight) {
                    newHeight = $element.data('expandedHeight') + 'px';
                    newLink = 'lessLink';
                    expanded = true;
                } else {
                    newHeight = collapsedHeight;
                    newLink = 'moreLink';
                }

                // Fire beforeToggle callback
                // Since we determined the new "expanded" state above we're now out of sync
                // with our true current state, so we need to flip the value of `expanded`
                if (this.options.beforeToggle && typeof this.options.beforeToggle === 'function') {
                    this.options.beforeToggle(trigger, $element, !expanded);
                }

                $element.css({
                    'height': newHeight
                });

                // Fire afterToggle callback
                $element.on('transitionend', (function (_this) {
                    return function () {
                        if (_this.options.afterToggle && typeof _this.options.afterToggle === 'function') {
                            _this.options.afterToggle(trigger, $element, expanded);
                        }

                        $(this).attr({
                            'aria-expanded': expanded
                        }).off('transitionend');
                    }
                })(this));

                $(trigger).replaceWith($(this.options[newLink])
                        .on('click', (function (_this) {
                            return function (event) {
                                _this.toggle(this, element, event);
                            };
                        })(this))
                        .attr({
                            'data-readmore-toggle': $element.attr('id'),
                            'aria-controls': $element.attr('id')
                        }));
            },

            destroy: function () {
                $(this.element).each(function () {
                    var current = $(this);

                    current.attr({
                        'data-readmore': null,
                        'aria-expanded': null
                    })
                            .css({
                                maxHeight: '',
                                height: ''
                            })
                            .next('[data-readmore-toggle]')
                            .remove();

                    current.removeData();
                });
            }
        };


        $.fn.readmore = function (options) {
            var args = arguments,
                    selector = this.selector;

            options = options || {};

            if (typeof options === 'object') {
                return this.each(function () {
                    if ($.data(this, 'plugin_' + readmore)) {
                        var instance = $.data(this, 'plugin_' + readmore);
                        instance.destroy.apply(instance);
                    }

                    options.selector = selector;

                    $.data(this, 'plugin_' + readmore, new Readmore(this, options));
                });
            } else if (typeof options === 'string' && options[0] !== '_' && options !== 'init') {
                return this.each(function () {
                    var instance = $.data(this, 'plugin_' + readmore);
                    if (instance instanceof Readmore && typeof instance[options] === 'function') {
                        instance[options].apply(instance, Array.prototype.slice.call(args, 1));
                    }
                });
            }
        };

    }));

    function subscribevalidation(obj,type,e)
    {

        if (e.keyCode == 9 || e.keyCode == 27 || (e.keyCode == 65) || (e.keyCode >= 35 && e.keyCode <= 39 ) || e.keyCode == 16|| e.keyCode == 32) {
            e.preventDefault();
            return false;
        }else if(e.keyCode == 13){
            $('.subscribe').click();
        }else{
            if(type=='subscribe_email'){
                if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
                {
                    $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);

                }else{
                    $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                }
                $("label[for='subscribe_email']").html('Email Address');
                $("label[for='subscribe_email']").css('color','');

            }else{
                if($.trim($(obj).val()).length==0)
                {
                    $(obj).addClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                }else{
                    $(obj).removeClass(INPUTFIELDCLASS);
                }
            }
        }

    }


    function loginvalidation(obj,type,e)
    {

        if (e.keyCode == 9 || e.keyCode == 27 || (e.keyCode == 65) || (e.keyCode >= 35 && e.keyCode <= 39 ) || e.keyCode == 16|| e.keyCode == 32) {

                }
            }
        }

    }

    function isVisible(ele) {
        if (ele != null) {
            return ele.style.opacity == '0' ? true : false;
        }
        return false;
    }
    function toggleVisibilityById(id) {
        var e = document.getElementById(id);
        toggleVisibility(e);
        //pushpingfun('0');
    }
    var topbar = 1;
    function toggleVisibility(element) {
        header = document.querySelector("header");

        if (isVisible(element)) {
            showElement1(element);
            $("#height-block").animate({height: "32"});
            topbar = 1;
            classie.add(header,"top32");

            if($('.product-catagory').hasClass('pinned'))
            {
                $('.product-catagory').animate({top: "32px"});
            }

        } else {
            hideElement1(element);
            $("#height-block").animate({height: "0px"});
            topbar = 0;
            classie.remove(header,"top32");

            $('.product-catagory').animate({top: "0px"});

        }
    }
    function hideElement1(ele) {
        /*document.getElementById("myImg").style.backgroundImage="url(//images.ganeshaspeaks.com/images_gsv7/icons/arrow-down2.png)";*/
        document.getElementById("arrowicon").classList.add('arrow-down-img');
        document.getElementById("arrowicon").classList.remove('arrow-up-img');

        ele.style.opacity = "0";
        ele.style.height = "0px";
    }

    function showElement1(ele) {
        /*document.getElementById("myImg").style.backgroundImage="url(//images.ganeshaspeaks.com/images_gsv7/icons/arrow-up2.png)";*/
        document.getElementById("arrowicon").classList.remove('arrow-down-img');
        document.getElementById("arrowicon").classList.add('arrow-up-img');
        ele.style.opacity = "1";
        ele.style.height = "33px";
    }

    /*header sticky script*/

    ( function( window ) {

        'use strict';


        function classReg( className ) {
            return new RegExp("(^|\\s+)" + className + "(\\s+|$)");
        }
        var hasClass, addClass, removeClass;

        if ( 'classList' in document.documentElement ) {
            hasClass = function( elem, c ) {
                return elem.classList.contains( c );
            };
            addClass = function( elem, c ) {
                elem.classList.add( c );
            };
            removeClass = function( elem, c ) {
                elem.classList.remove( c );
            };
        }
        else {
            hasClass = function( elem, c ) {
                return classReg( c ).test( elem.className );
            };
            addClass = function( elem, c ) {
                if ( !hasClass( elem, c ) ) {
                    elem.className = elem.className + ' ' + c;
                }
            };
            removeClass = function( elem, c ) {
                elem.className = elem.className.replace( classReg( c ), ' ' );
            };
        }

        function toggleClass( elem, c ) {
            var fn = hasClass( elem, c ) ? removeClass : addClass;
            fn( elem, c );
        }

        var classie = {
            // full names
            hasClass: hasClass,
            addClass: addClass,
            removeClass: removeClass,
            toggleClass: toggleClass,
            // short names
            has: hasClass,
            add: addClass,
            remove: removeClass,
            toggle: toggleClass
        };

// transport
        if ( typeof define === 'function' && define.amd ) {
            // AMD
            define( classie );
        } else {
            // browser global
            window.classie = classie;
        }

    })( window );

    if(stickystoreMylifeVar==1){
    }else{
        if(topbaradjavascritp==1){
            function init1() {
                window.addEventListener('scroll', function(e){
                    var distanceY = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop,
                            shrinkOn = 0,
                            header = document.querySelector("header");
                    topheaderfix = document.querySelector("body");


                    if (distanceY > shrinkOn) {
                                                classie.add(header,"smaller"),classie.add(topheaderfix,"body-padding");
                        if(remove_second_sticky_top==1){
                            classie.add(header,"smaller"),classie.add(topheaderfix,"body-padding1");
                        }
                        if(topbar == 1) {
                            //classie.add(header,"top32");
                        } else {
                            //classie.remove(header,"top32");
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (classie.has(header,"smaller"),(topheaderfix,"body-padding")) {
                            classie.remove(header,"smaller");
                            classie.remove(topheaderfix,"body-padding");
                            if(remove_second_sticky_top==1){
                            classie.remove(topheaderfix,"body-padding1");
                            }
                        }
                        if (classie.has(topheaderfix,"top226")) {
                            classie.remove(topheaderfix,"top226");
                        }
                        if(topbar == 1) {
                            // classie.add(header,"top32");
                        } else {
                            //classie.remove(header,"top32");
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
            init1();
        }else{
            function init() {
                window.addEventListener('scroll', function(e){
                    var distanceY = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop,
                            shrinkOn = 0,
                            header = document.querySelector("header");
                    topheaderfix = document.querySelector("body");


                    if (distanceY > shrinkOn) {
                                            classie.add(header,"smaller"),classie.add(topheaderfix,"body-padding");
                        if(remove_second_sticky_top==1){
                            classie.add(header,"smaller"),classie.add(topheaderfix,"body-padding1");
                        }
                        if(topbar == 1) {
                            classie.add(header,"top32");
                        } else {
                            classie.remove(header,"top32");
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (classie.has(header,"smaller"),(topheaderfix,"body-padding")) {
                            classie.remove(header,"smaller");
                            classie.remove(topheaderfix,"body-padding");
                            if(remove_second_sticky_top==1){
                            classie.remove(topheaderfix,"body-padding1");
                            }
                        }
                        if (classie.has(topheaderfix,"top226")) {
                            classie.remove(topheaderfix,"top226");
                        }    
                        if(topbar == 1) {
                            classie.add(header,"top32");
                        } else {
                            classie.remove(header,"top32");
                        }
                    }
                });

            }
            init();

        }
    }
